# Seed 47 on the AMD Ryzen 7 PRO 8840HS - small: every measure inside the rule, hypervolume +1.4% (Iteration 269)

`seed 47 tag2 6h then experiment compare-fronts.py --rounds 6 --frontier
--cpus 2,8`, commit c0bf29d4 (Iteration 248: the after-seed-46 batch -
primitives together first and PRIMITIVE? by address, the classic headers back,
bit 7 of the count byte free, POSTPONE deciding immediacy when it compiles,
>NAME to coreext.4 (reseeded), the gene jittail, the tail engines compiling
again), started 13:15; the evolution 13:15-17:29 (4 h 14 min - the owner's 6
hours, generations 0-89), the front measured again by 17:43, the comparison
packed by 19:04. 22,642 designs made, 18,674 measured (18,549 alive), 3,968
screened - not measured.

The comparison's own verdict (five measures): fastest +2.5%, smallest +0.05%,
memory -2 KB, hypervolume +1.4%, best Q -2.1% - **small**, the first since seed
44 (45 and 46 were not). The stopping rule would count it the first of two, but
the after-seed-47 batch brings new genes (jitk, lto, gotodup, compiler, factor):
it counts afresh from seed 48. The Q-best design of all runs is seed 43's
19510c7689: 0.205 at 25,583 bytes, 172 KB (Q 0.277 of s6's); the run's own
Q-best 037722ab8a, 0.174 at 26,616 bytes, 180 KB (Q 0.254 as the run measured
it). Calibration 0.997 on cpu 2, 0.988 on cpu 8; cpu 2 against cpu 8 median
1.004, Spearman 1.00.

From the run's database (Iteration 269, lab/evolve/seed-facts.py):

- **Deaths: 125, all "reach limit at scale 0 - not run"** (refused before
  running). No engine or corpus death: the tail engines compiled again - 626
  designs with tail lived - and none of them reached the front.
- The screen: 3,968 of the 21,128 children from generation 5 skipped (18.8%).
- Children by the try that made them: mutation 56.1%, crossover 32.3%, borrowed
  11.6% (the last fifth: crossover 35.4%).
- Via the attic: 3,398 children; 9 of the 124 on the final front.
- The final front, 124 designs: mutation 85, borrowed 19, carried 13, crossover
  7.
- The small third (41 designs, 15,808-17,305 bytes): fsys 3 on 39, 4 on 2;
  threads 3 on all; klookup on none. The fast third (23,517-34,029): fsys 2 on
  38, 1 on 3; klookup on 25.
- The JIT on 44 of the front (jitx and jitloop on all 44, jitinl on 36,
  **jittail on 27**, jitos on 8); tos on 115; guard on 14.
- fbuf: 4 KB buffers on 70, 1 KB on 30, 512 bytes on 24.
- In the run: fastest e141550b9a 0.164 at 34,029 (192 KB); smallest 7535695a36
  15,808 (speed 1.449, 132 KB) - 64 bytes under seed 46's smallest (15,872): the
  batch's smaller images; least memory 70fe89d744, 128 KB (15,816 bytes).
- A try from the front repeats a design the run made 29.9% of the time by
  mutation alone, 0.7% by crossover.

---

# Evolved VM designs

18674 designs evaluated, 18549 alive, 3968 screened - not measured (Iteration 200). Speed is the geometric mean of median cpu time / hand-made s6, long workloads (Iteration 100) over kernel, fib, corpus, loop, bubble, ackermann,
relative to s6-cv8b; size is the self-hosting image, binary the stripped engine. sieve, parse is held out.
The front is over three objectives, equal (Iteration 75): speed, binary + image, peak memory - memory within 8 KB a tie (Iteration 77).


The Q-best design (binary + image x run time x memory - the knee; information): 037722ab8a, speed 0.174, binary + image 26,616, memory 180 KB - Q 0.254 of s6-cv8b's.

## The front's genomes

Each design above, and any other the front was measured again with, as one JSON line: its record without the raw timings. A tool that takes a database (--db) reads this report as one - the lines that are not JSON are skipped - so results/ is all a new chat needs to rebuild a front design.
